| mood | 2/10 | The novel presents a bleak, pessimistic view of Gilded Age society where social constraints and economic pressures lead to inevitable tragedy. |
| spice_level | 2/10 | Romance exists but is understated and proper, focusing on social compatibility and marriage prospects rather than physical passion or explicit content. |
| world_building | 8/10 | Wharton creates an immersive and meticulously detailed portrait of New York's high society, with its intricate social rules, economic structures, and cultural expectations. |
